- **Vue文件代码排版**: Vue 单文件组件固定按以下顺序组织： ([lengyibai/tool](https://github.com/lengyibai/tool/blob/HEAD/agents/Vue%E6%96%87%E4%BB%B6%E4%BB%A3%E7%A0%81%E6%8E%92%E7%89%88.md))
- **Vue规范**: - Vue 组件统一使用 Vue 3 Composition API 和 `&lt;script setup lang="ts"&gt;`。 - 单文件组件顺序固定为 `&lt;script&gt;`、`&lt;template&gt;`、`&lt;style&gt;`。 - `&lt;template&gt;` 只能保留一个根节点，不允许多个并列根标签或组件。 - 页面结构、文本和原生标签的使用遵循 [HTML 规范](./HTML规范.md)；组件标签使用 PascalCase。 - Props 只读，不在子组件中直接修改；双向绑定使用 `defineModel`。 - 自定义事件在脚本类型和模板监听中统一使用 kebab-case，例如 `tab-change`。 - 变量名与属性名一致时使用绑定简写 `:xxx`；布尔属性为 `true` 时直接写属性名。 - 不在模板表达式中使用 `as` 类型断言，也不调用会修改状态、发请求或产生其他副 ([lengyibai/tool](https://github.com/lengyibai/tool/blob/HEAD/agents/Vue%E8%A7%84%E8%8C%83.md))
- **vulcan**: Subject Matter Auditor for podcast pipeline. Reviews scripts for technical accuracy and quality. Use as the quality gate before audio production. ([idallasjlabs/agentshroud](https://github.com/idallasjlabs/agentshroud/blob/HEAD/.omp/agents/vulcan.md) · 4 copies)
- **Vulcan-AWS**: Vulcan-AWS C# Agent — sviluppo cloud-native su AWS con .NET 10 LTS: Lambda, DynamoDB, SQS, SNS, S3, ECS, API Gateway, CDK. Usare per GENERARE codice C# con target AWS. Per codice provider-agnostic usare Vulcan-Core, per Azure usare Vulcan-Azure. Per CODE REVIEW usare Anubis. ([LuPaLa-Coder/Vulcan](https://github.com/LuPaLa-Coder/Vulcan/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/vulcan-aws.md))

Usare per GENERARE codice C# con target Azure. Per codice provider-agnostic usare Vulcan-Core, per AWS usare Vulcan-AWS. Per CODE REVIEW usare Anubis. - **vzt-architect**: Planning agent (Opus 5.5 @ max effort) — the `opus@max` rung. Architecture, technical specs, roadmaps, migration plans, PRD breakdown, and approach selection for systems that have prior art to reason from. Use for ALL routine planning; escalate to vzt-planner (Fable) only when the design is genuinely novel. Read-only: produces a plan, never edits files. ([vonzelle-vzt/vzt-agent-protocol](https://github.com/vonzelle-vzt/vzt-agent-protocol/blob/HEAD/agents/vzt-architect.md) · ★2)
- **vzt-art-director**: Visual authoring agent (Opus 5.5) — writes a repo's DESIGN.md from its real token layer, and owns visual work that requires taste nobody has written down yet: establishing a look, a brand pass, 'make this feel premium'. Use when NO DESIGN.md exists. Once one does, applying it is execution work — route that to 'vzt-stylist' (Sonnet). Not for technical/architectural design: that is 'vzt-architect'. ([vonzelle-vzt/vzt-agent-protocol](https://github.com/vonzelle-vzt/vzt-agent-protocol/blob/HEAD/agents/vzt-art-director.md) · ★2)
- **vzt-builder**: Standard execution agent (Sonnet 5.5) — the default builder. Implements features, fixes bugs, writes tests, wires endpoints/components/CRUD, executes plan steps tagged tier: sonnet. Use for all routine implementation; it burns the Sonnet-only usage bucket and preserves Opus/Fable quota. ([vonzelle-vzt/vzt-agent-protocol](https://github.com/vonzelle-vzt/vzt-agent-protocol/blob/HEAD/agents/vzt-builder.md) · ★2)
- **vzt-heavy-builder**: Heavy implementation agent (Opus 5.5) — large refactors, migrations, dense algorithmic work, performance surgery, concurrency, multi-file changes with tight coupling. Use when the change is too gnarly for the standard builder, or when a plan step is tagged tier: opus. ([vonzelle-vzt/vzt-agent-protocol](https://github.com/vonzelle-vzt/vzt-agent-protocol/blob/HEAD/agents/vzt-heavy-builder.md) · ★2)
- **vzt-mechanic**: Mechanical-edit agent (Haiku 5.5) — renames, typo fixes, version bumps, formatting, lint fixes, import sorting, dead-code removal, changelog entries, file moves, commit messages. Use PROACTIVELY for any edit that requires no judgment so premium tiers never burn quota on it. ([vonzelle-vzt/vzt-agent-protocol](https://github.com/vonzelle-vzt/vzt-agent-protocol/blob/HEAD/agents/vzt-mechanic.md) · ★2)
- **vzt-oracle**: Frontier debugging agent (Fable 5.1) — root-cause analysis for impossible bugs: race conditions, heisenbugs, corruption, intermittent failures, cross-system mysteries, security holes. Use when cheaper tiers have failed twice or the bug defies reproduction. Read-only diagnosis; hands the fix to a builder. ([vonzelle-vzt/vzt-agent-protocol](https://github.com/vonzelle-vzt/vzt-agent-protocol/blob/HEAD/agents/vzt-oracle.md) · ★2)
- **vzt-planner**: Frontier planning agent (Fable 5.1) — the LAST rung, for planning with no prior art to reason from: novel or greenfield architecture, from-scratch system design, one-way-door distributed-systems / multi-tenancy / sharding decisions. Do NOT use for routine architecture, tech specs, roadmaps, or migration plans — those belong to 'vzt-architect' (Opus 5.5 @ max), which is half the cost. Read-only: produces a plan, never edits files. ([vonzelle-vzt/vzt-agent-protocol](https://github.com/vonzelle-vzt/vzt-agent-protocol/blob/HEAD/agents/vzt-planner.md) · ★2)
- **vzt-reviewer**: Load-bearing review agent (Opus 5.5) — deep code review, security review, pre-merge audit of the risky seam. Use on the one or two steps a plan flags as load-bearing, or when the user asks for a thorough review. Read-only: reports findings, does not fix. ([vonzelle-vzt/vzt-agent-protocol](https://github.com/vonzelle-vzt/vzt-agent-protocol/blob/HEAD/agents/vzt-reviewer.md) · ★2)
- **vzt-scout**: Recon agent (Haiku 5.5) — codebase search, file location, dependency lookups, summaries, status checks, 'where is X / find all Y / how many Z' questions. Use PROACTIVELY for any read-only discovery so premium tiers never burn quota on grep work. Read-only. ([vonzelle-vzt/vzt-agent-protocol](https://github.com/vonzelle-vzt/vzt-agent-protocol/blob/HEAD/agents/vzt-scout.md) · ★2)
- **vzt-stylist**: Visual execution agent (Sonnet 5.5) — applies an existing DESIGN.md to real UI: restyles, spacing/typography/palette fixes, dark mode, responsive work. Use when the repo HAS a DESIGN.md; the taste is already on disk and this agent's job is to apply it faithfully. If there is no DESIGN.md, the taste has to be authored first — that is 'vzt-art-director' (Opus). ([vonzelle-vzt/vzt-agent-protocol](https://github.com/vonzelle-vzt/vzt-agent-protocol/blob/HEAD/agents/vzt-stylist.md) · ★2)
